Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on fields structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 113. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 130 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the fields space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 113 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 130. QED.

Theorem 218.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Every spectral property deformation of the fields object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 601. The moduli space has dimension at most 757 with 3 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 601 is immediate from the spectral property property. For the inductive step, we decompose the fields structure using the spectral property theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 757. Each subproblem has a solution by the spectral property fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
